To reduce future infestations, the farmer should rotate maize with non-host plants like groundnut, deep plough and bury or burn infested maize residues, and keep the field well-weeded [APNI-SARI-2023-MAIZE:p20-faw-prevention]. For current infestations, the farmer should consult the local Agricultural Extension Agent (AEA) for advice on recommended fall army worm control pesticides in the area [APNI-SARI-2023-MAIZE:p20-faw-control]."}

If an infestation is detected, the officer should advise the farmer to consult their local Agricultural Extension Agent (AEA) for advice on recommended fall armyworm control pesticides [APNI-SARI-2023-MAIZE:p20-faw-control]."}
